{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"helms","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The tiller (or, in a large ship, the wheel) which is used to steer the rudder of a marine vessel; also, the entire steering apparatus of a vessel."],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["The use of a helm (sense 1); also, the amount of space through which a helm is turned."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["The member of a vessel's crew in charge of steering the vessel; a helmsman or helmswoman."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["rare"],"word":"helmsperson"}],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["nautical","transport"]},{"glosses":["Something used to control or steer; also (obsolete), a handle of a tool or weapon; a haft, a helve."],"tags":["broadly"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the helm of the Commonwealth","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A position of control or leadership."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"glosses":["One in the position of controlling or directing; a controller, a director, a guide."],"tags":["figuratively"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"hĕlm","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/hɛlm/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":253538,"upstream_offset":1258611911,"upstream_record_sha256":"dcf136f559305beed173bc9c6381eb9956fd4ebb1c59a1028ad4dc5bb627017d","word":"helm"}
